   - N-C-B-F-T
   - N-C-E-A

- Removing (N, B) and (Q, I) will disconnect all paths between N and Q.
  - Paths N-B-Q and N-B-F-T-G-I-Q are immediately blocked.

   - E -> B -> C -> J
   - E -> B -> R -> H -> J
   - E -> B -> R -> Q -> J
   - E -> B -> A -> N -> O -> G -> I -> Q -> J
   - E -> B -> A -> N -> O -> G -> H -> J
   - E -> S -> F -> T -> Q -> J
   - E -> S -> F -> T -> R -> Q -> J
   - E -> S -> F -> T -> R -> H -> J

- Removing H and Q would effectively disconnect all paths as they are key nodes in many of the paths connecting E and J.

By removing nodes H and Q, all paths connecting E and J are cut off.

### Conclusion:

--- Model Response ---
To solve the problem of disconnecting the two black nodes (E and J), we need to identify the minimum number of nodes that, when removed, will ensure there are no paths between E and J.

### Step-by-Step Analysis

1. **Identify Paths Between E and J:**
   - Direct connections from E: E → A, E → B, E → S, E → F
   - Direct connections from J: J → G, J → I, J → Q, J → H

2. **Intermediate Nodes Connecting E and J:**
   - E → B → C → G → J
   - E → B → C → N → G → J
   - E → B → C → O → Q → J
   - E → S → B → C → G → J
   - E → S → B → C → N → G → J
   - E → S → B → C → O → Q → J
   - E → F → T → B → C → G → J
   - E → F → T → B → C → N → G → J
   - E → F → T → B → C → O → Q → J

3. **Identify Cut Sets:**
   - Removing B disconnects the majority of paths from E to J.
   - Removing C disconnects the paths from E to J through B.
   - Removing G disconnects paths from J through C.
   - Removing T ensures no path from E through F.

4. **Minimum Cut Set:**
   - Removing B and G will disconnect E and J.
   - Removing B and C will also disconnect E and J.

### Conclusion

The minimum number of nodes to remove to completely disconnect the two black nodes is 2. The possible cut sets are [B, G] or [B, C].

   - Removing edge \( (C, G) \) will cut paths 1-4.
   - Removing edge \( (J, I) \) will ensure additional isolation due to involvement in many paths.

Therefore, the minimum number of edges that need to be removed is 2.
